## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can get a broken test in one of your repos fixed while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 맡길 수 있어요. 예를 들어 "이 저장소에서 실패하는 테스트를 고쳐 줘"처럼 말씀해 주시면 제가 작업을 열어서 진행하고, 끝나면 결과를 알려드릴게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 좋아요, 내일 아침에 봐요. 편하게 쉬세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP と UDP の一番の違いは、**TCP は届いたことと順番を保証し、UDP は保証せず速さを優先する**点です。

TCP は到達確認や再送を行うので、信頼性が必要なWeb閲覧やファイル転送に向いています。UDP は再送などをしない分軽く速いため、動画配信やオンラインゲームのように多少の欠落より遅延を避けたい場面で使われます。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para limpiar tu rama local antes de compartirla: ordenar, aplastar o reescribir commits que aún no subiste. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es pública o la usan otros, porque reescribir historia compartida rompe el trabajo de quien la tiene.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、可靠的：先握手建立连接，保证数据不丢、不乱、按序到达；UDP 则不建连接、不保证送达，速度快、开销小。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase to tidy your own unpushed commits before sharing them, for a linear history. Merge when the branch is already shared or you want the real history of when work came together, and never rebase commits others have built on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径直接跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

不用写全路径也可以，`bun test foo` 会跑所有文件名里含 `foo` 的测试。想只跑某一个用例，加 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：

```
bun test test/foo.test.ts -t "某个用例的名字"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file that records every change before it's applied to the main data, so the database can replay or roll back those changes after a crash. It lets the database stay fast and safe, because writes go to a quick sequential log first and the slower data files get updated later.
```

### rich-compare-en

```
--- step 1: user says: Put these side by side for me. Mac mini M4: $599, 16 GB, always plugged in, stays at home. MacBook Air M4: $999, 16 GB, runs on battery, comes with me. Which is the better always-on home for you?
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我接着 compaction 那个 PR，把 review 提到的锁粒度改细，弄好了告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户说："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

要做的：
- 找到 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把锁的范围缩小（只锁真正需要保护的部分，减少 compaction 期间的阻塞）。
- 在现有分支上提交，不新开分支。
- 跑相关测试，确认没有回归；并发相关的改动要有覆盖。

完成标准：
- 锁粒度改动已提交到当前分支；
- 报告里给出测试命令和结果，以及改了哪几处锁。

不要做的：
- 不合并、不推送、不改 PR 的状态（推送和更新 PR 等用户点头再说）。
- 不动 review 之外的逻辑。
```

```
}
```

```
(earlier) user: Run hawdb's write benchmark every night and tell me how it went.
(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.
(earlier) partner: Night 3: 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, level with last night; no compaction stalls.

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] 好，看板做好了，把每晚的写入结果都放在这里，新一晚跑完会自动更新。
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly writes", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1792 characters): <main class="k-app">
<section class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="wps">–</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">writes/s</div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<p class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>No nightly runs yet.</p>
</main>
<script>
function fmt(n){return n>=1000?Math.round(n/1000)+'k':String(n);}
function render(nights){
  nights = Array.isArray(nights) ? nights : [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  document.getElementById('empty').hidden = nights.length > 0;
  if (!nights.length) { document.getElementById('wps').textContent = '–'; document.getElementById('sub').textContent = 'writes/s'; return; }
  var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
  document.getElementById('wps').textContent = fmt(last.wps) + ' writes/s';
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' ms · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls;
  nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function(r){
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = 'Night ' + r.n;
    var sub = document.createElement('div');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + r.p99 + ' ms · stalls ' + r.stalls;
    var val = document.createElement('div');
    val.className = 'k-row-value';
    val.textContent = fmt(r.wps) + '/s';
    var text = document.createElement('div');
    text.appendChild(main); text.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(text); row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
render(ducter.get('nights'));
ducter.onChange(function(){ render(ducter.get('nights')); });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"n":2,"wps":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":0},{"n":3,"wps":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}
```

```
}

$('save').onclick = function(){
  var name = $('name').value.trim();
  var amt = parseFloat($('amt').value);
  if (!(amt > 0)) return;
  if (editing !== null) {
    items = items.map(function(i){
      return i.id === editing ? { id: i.id, day: $('day').value, name: name, amount: amt } : i;
    });
  } else {
    items.push({ id: Date.now(), day: $('day').value, name: name, amount: amt });
  }
  items.sort(function(a, b){ return a.day < b.day ? -1 : a.day > b.day ? 1 : 0; });
  save();
  resetForm();
  render();
  $('addbox').open = false;
};

$('cancel').onclick = function(){ resetForm(); };

$('del').onclick = function(){
  if (!delArmed) {
    delArmed = true;
    $('del').textContent = '再点一次删除';
    return;
  }
  items = items.filter(function(i){ return i.id !== editing; });
  save();
  resetForm();
  render();
  $('addbox').open = false;
};

$('addday').onclick = function(){
  var all = allDays();
  var last = all[all.length - 1];
  var x = new Date(last + 'T00:00:00');
  x.setDate(x.getDate() + 1);
  var next = ymd(x);
  if (days.indexOf(next) < 0) days.push(next);
  save();
  fillDaySelect(next);
  render();
};

ducter.onChange(function(){ load(); });
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
  [also used tools] app
```

```
}
```

```
<div class="k-h">花销明细</div>

<div class="k-card">
<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" value="北京出差 11/20–21"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-20"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>分类</label><div class="k-chips" id="cats"><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="true" data-c="交通">交通</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="住宿">住宿</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="餐饮">餐饮</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="其他">其他</button></div></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</div></details>
</main>
<script>
(function(){
const KEY="items";
let cat="交通";
const $=id=>document.getElementById( …    main.className="k-row-main";
    main.textContent=x.trip;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");
    sub.className="k-row-sub";
    const d=document.createElement("span"); d.textContent=x.day||"";
    const c=document.createElement("span"); c.textContent=x.cat||"";
    sub.append(d,c);
    const val=document.createElement("div");
    val.className="k-row-value";
    val.textContent=fmt(x.amt);
    row.append(main,sub,val);
    row.onclick=()=>{
      if(row.dataset.arm){
        const ys=items(); ys.splice(i,1); ducter.set(KEY,ys);
      } else {
        row.dataset.arm="1"; sub.textContent="再点一下删除";
      }
    };
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total));
}
document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
  cat=b.dataset.c;
  document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(c=>c.setAttribute("aria-pressed",c===b?"true":"false"));
});
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=Number($("amt").value);
  if(!amt) return;
  const xs=items();
  xs.push({trip:$("trip").value.trim()||"出差",day:$("day").value.trim(),cat:cat,amt:amt});
  ducter.set(KEY,xs);
  $("amt").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 上海虹桥→北京南 07:00"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚"}]}
```
